# Vtables

Generated by `.claude/skills/find-reversing-targets/scripts/catalogues.py` from the IDB exports, gta_sa.exe and the repository. **Do not edit by hand**; regenerate it (see the script's docstring) when the repo changes.

385 classes registered with `RH_ScopedVirtualClass(cls, vtable, slots)`. Slots are read from gta_sa.exe. A slot is listed under a class only when its address differs from the base class's vtable (the base is taken from the class declaration). Use this to check `RH_ScopedVMTInstall` addresses: an address that is not in the class's vtable makes `VMTInfo` throw at start-up.

Own slots across all classes: 1299 reversed, 160 stub, 834 not hooked.
